# Plan status

Generated by `scripts/generate-site-data` from `.ai/repo/project/`. Do not edit; edit the
canonical records and regenerate. `majordomus plan status` prints the same thing at a terminal,
and the website renders it at `/plan/`. Semantics are in [`PLANNING.md`](PLANNING.md).

Generated from canonical inputs `4290ce40066a`.

| [M000](https://majordomus.dev/plan/m000/) | DONE | 15/15 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | Every non-trivial change in this repository belongs to a milestone specification and to one dependency-linked issue execution contract, validated as a DAG, with status derived rather than asserted, and projected to GitHub and to the website from that one canonical model. |
| [M001](https://majordomus.dev/plan/m001/) | DONE | 4/4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | An unresolved question keeps refusing a completed finish after the work moves to a new task, or the alternative is chosen deliberately and the reason is recorded where the next worker will read it. |
| [M002](https://majordomus.dev/plan/m002/) | ACTIVE | 2/4 | 1 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 | A durable record either names the worker that produced it or is refused, and a record written from one session can never arrive attributed to another session's task. |
| [M003](https://majordomus.dev/plan/m003/) | ACTIVE | 12/16 | 2 | 2 | 0 | 0 | 1 | A worker with no conversation history can answer what happened, why, what was decided, which evidence supports it, where the canonical source lives, and what to do next — from records and a derived index, with git still the authority over both. |
| [M004](https://majordomus.dev/plan/m004/) | ACTIVE | 19/42 | 12 | 11 | 0 | 0 | 0 | Every public command can be benchmarked from the command registry with cold and warm distributions, the phases and work counters of any run can be printed, the caches and batch reads that remove repeated work preserve every observable result, an accepted baseline lives in the repository and a check refuses a regression by policy, and the pre-commit hook is fast enough that nobody routes around it. |
| [a-change-is-judged-in-minutes](https://majordomus.dev/plan/a-change-is-judged-in-minutes/) | PLANNED | 0/11 | 3 | 8 | 0 | 0 | 0 | The owner should never wait on the toolchain for a change that touched little. An ordinary derive finishes in under a second. A pull request's required verdict arrives within five minutes. Both are measured and enforced, and the speed comes from not repeating work whose inputs did not move, never from weakening a gate. |
| [a-claim-stands-only-on-its-evidence](https://majordomus.dev/plan/a-claim-stands-only-on-its-evidence/) | PLANNED | 0/18 | 14 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | The owner wants every rule, guarantee and delivered feature that Majordomus states to be backed by a recorded execution at the revision being shown. For a blocking rule, that execution shows a violation being refused. Every way around a gate (an override, a disabled protection, a proposed decision cited as law) must be visible. Any statement that lacks this evidence reads as not proven on every surface, never as done. |
| [a-derivation-computed-once-is-reused](https://majordomus.dev/plan/a-derivation-computed-once-is-reused/) | BLOCKED | 0/5 | 0 | 5 | 0 | 0 | 0 | A node's outputs are kept under its key outside every work tree, restored only after their digests are verified, bounded in size and age, and shared by the worktrees of one machine and by CI runs. The incremental path is the default only after the differential case, the measurements and a rollback case exist, and scripts/derive --full remains. |

A trunk run reaches the ledger through a reviewed recording pull request, and a supplementary record (a run record, coverage, a local row) can only weaken a verdict, naming itself when it does. Verified is rendered only for the one proven row of the freshness table, on every surface. |

| [two-folder-integration](https://majordomus.dev/plan/two-folder-integration/) | ACTIVE | 1/23 | 2 | 20 | 0 | 0 | 0 | Adoption is a reconciliation, not a template copy. One typed repository model is discovered once and every consumer reads it. A desired integration is computed from that model, diffed against what the repository actually holds, and applied as the smallest mutation that closes the gap — with everything Majordomus owns living under `.ai/` and `.majordomus/`, and anything outside those two namespaces reduced to a small managed bridge whose ownership is recorded. Running the reconciliation twice changes nothing the second time, and uninstalling gives back exactly what was taken and nothing a person authored. |
| [web-sign-in](https://majordomus.dev/plan/web-sign-in/) | PLANNED | 0/8 | 1 | 7 | 0 | 0 | 0 | One canonical identity-provider kind under .ai/repo/identity/providers/ describes each provider as data: its issuer, its endpoints or its discovery document, the scopes requested, the claim that carries a stable subject and the environment variable names its credentials arrive in. A single OpenID-Connect-shaped flow in the executable serves every provider in that directory; Google and Facebook are two objects, not two code paths. Sessions are cookie-borne, signed, short and revocable; identity is attached to a request once, in the router, and every surface — HTTP, Cockpit, OpenAPI, MCP — reads it from the same place. The capability registry's effect classification, not a per-route list, decides what an anonymous reader may do, and the OpenAPI document and the Cockpit both state that decision because both project it from the registry. Striking a provider is deleting one file and regenerating. |
| [what-a-workspace-decides-reaches-the-repository-with-its-source](https://majordomus.dev/plan/what-a-workspace-decides-reaches-the-repository-with-its-source/) | BLOCKED | 0/2 | 0 | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 | The owner thinks aloud in the ChatGPT project. What the owner decides there is read into this checkout's own store, promoted by a person into intents, ADRs, milestones, issues and knowledge, and every promoted record names the conversation and message it came from, so that from the UI one click opens that message for the operator who holds the store, while the public repository and site carry only the reference. |
| [work-graph-github](https://majordomus.dev/plan/work-graph-github/) | ACTIVE | 10/11 | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | The projection cannot rot silently: a gate runs the drift check on every change that can move the model or the adapter, and drift is a CI failure with the record named. Identity travels with the record rather than being parsed out of its title, so renaming a GitHub title moves nothing and creates no duplicate, and the remote can be a fixture, so which record is which is provable with no network. The reconciliation vocabulary distinguishes the states it currently conflates, so `missing` no longer stands for both a record never created and a record a person deleted, and a remote edit is reported as a remote edit. Above the projection, a pull request names the issue it realises and the edge is derived from git and GitHub rather than remembered, so a merged commit leads back to the acceptance criteria it satisfied and an outcome leads forward to the evidence that it holds. The derivations the shell already computes — ready, blocked, waves, next, status — are readable through the same capability surface as every other thing this repository knows, rather than only from a terminal. |

- `I3766` (public-proof, wave 0) — The install section links each page once, and the recorded refusals are grouped by the use case they prove
- `I3767` (surfaces-never-disagree, wave 0) — The language switch of a UI or Cockpit page keeps the reader on that page, and says so when the surface has no copy in the language chosen
- `I3769` (truthful-plan-surfaces, wave 0) — A published page reads delivery at the commit it was built from, and names one commit
- `I3770` (the-mesh-works-out-of-the-box, wave 0) — A server that lost its checkout lease answers every mesh read by naming the server that holds it, never with an empty mesh
- `I3771` (mesh-journal-converges, wave 0) — A claim scope that is not a repository path is refused, or mapped to the record file it names, so a milestone claim excludes what it says
- `I3772` (mesh-journal-converges, wave 0) — Every mesh link counts its failed sync rounds, and a link whose rounds fail does not read plainly connected
- `I3774` (every-session-joins-the-mesh, wave 0) — Every completed step of a session is announced on the mesh by the step itself, and the board, the monitor and the UI show it
- `I3775` (derived-data-integrity, wave 0) — A page written by hand is never resolved as derived: the merge=derived globs name only what a generator writes
- `I3820` (fly-deployment, wave 0) — A deployment names the provider that hosts it, and the provider's files are generated from it
- `I3823` (an-authorised-node-connects-from-anywhere, wave 0) — The mesh client reaches an https endpoint over TLS and refuses cleartext to a public address
- `I3824` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — The cooperation rule says a worker lands its own green head, and every bootstrap says the same
- `I3829` (a-provider-carries-the-contract-natively, wave 0) — A provider declares whether its client drives a browser beside the server, and preflight says when this machine's bridge is missing
- `I3830` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — A local gate reports and CI decides: pre-commit and pre-push refuse only on integrity
- `I3831` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — A hook never starts a cold cargo build: a stale executable is the installed release, or the check is skipped and says so
- `I3832` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — majordomus worktree sweep removes merged, clean, idle worktrees, never with force, and announces each on the mesh
- `I3833` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — majordomus disk prune reclaims the container runtime's caches by name: build cache, anonymous unused volumes, dangling images, then trims the VM disk
- `I3834` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — A case run removes its own scratch dir however it ends: passed, failed, timed out or interrupted
- `I3835` (the-mesh-works-out-of-the-box, wave 0) — A node the mesh lists as trusted and present but cannot link is said so, with why, on every surface that names it
- `I3836` (development-runtime, wave 0) — An offload derive observes the bundle's base commit, not its throwaway snapshot, so what it derives passes CI's check
- `I3838` (a-change-is-judged-in-minutes, wave 0) — The plan page renders within the audit budget at every width, because a finding is counted rather than printed once per pair
- `I3839` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — A build that cannot take its build directory's lock never falls back to a cold private directory below the free-disk bound: it waits, bounded, or refuses naming the bound and the holder
- `I3841` (a-change-is-judged-in-minutes, wave 0) — The plan's graph is drawn by milestone, so /plan/dag/ answers the site probe within its budget however large the backlog grows
- `I3910` (landing-survives-any-session, wave 0) — The integration executor fast-forwards master to every green head, a combined top included, and nothing else lands
- `I3911` (landing-survives-any-session, wave 0) — The executor reads its audit trail, its halt and its lease from origin, so any clone continues it and a stale holder is refused
- `I3916` (truthful-plan-surfaces, wave 0) — The homepage counts supported claims from the run the evidence page reads, and names the revision it was recorded at
- `I3917` (truthful-plan-surfaces, wave 0) — The plan's status counts leave the homepage hero and stay on the roadmap
- `I3918` (truthful-plan-surfaces, wave 0) — README, CONTRIBUTING and the homepage say truthfully how big the tool is and what its registry counts are
- `I3919` (ci-critical-path, wave 0) — The coverage gate finds the end of a test module across multi-line and raw string literals
- `I4000` (a-session-proceeds-through-the-mesh, wave 0) — A repeated executions.start with the same key answers one execution
- `I4002` (a-session-proceeds-through-the-mesh, wave 0) — Worktree.create is a typed writing capability
- `I4006` (a-session-proceeds-through-the-mesh, wave 0) — Machine.slot is a counting machine-wide slot for heavy work
- `I4007` (a-session-proceeds-through-the-mesh, wave 0) — An idle serve exits by itself after a bound
- `I4008` (a-session-proceeds-through-the-mesh, wave 0) — A push that only deletes refs runs no plan in the pre-push hook
